372                                                             Figure 2behaviour of coffee agroforestry basin in Costa Rica F. Gómez-Delgado et al.: Modelling hydrological [SOURCE: Page_5]

coordinates −83◦ 440 3900 and −83◦ 430 3500 (West longitude), and between 9◦ 560 800 and 9◦ 560 3500 (North latitude) and is homogeneously planted with coffee (Coffea arabica L., var Caturra) on bare soil, shaded by free-growing tall Erythrina poeppigiana trees. The initial planting density for coffee was 6300 plants ha−1 , with a current age >30 years, 20% canopy openness and 2.5 m canopy height. It is intensively man- aged and selectively pruned (20% per year, around March). Shade trees have a density of 12.8 trees ha−1 , with 12.3% canopy cover and 20 m canopy height. The experimental basin has an area of 0.9 km^2 , an elevation range from 1020       Fig. 2. Mean monthly rainfall at Aquiares farm station (period up to 1280 m a.s.l. and a mean slope of 20%.
